1						;--------------------------------
2						; FILE>MOON.CODE.1
3						;--------------------------------
4	.0860	78		sei		COLD_START SEI ; TURN OFF INTERRUPTS
5	.0861	20 37 0e	jsr $0e37	JSR INIT_IO ; SETUP I/O HARDWARE
6	.0864	a9 00		lda #$00	LDA #$00
7	.0866	aa		tax		TAX
8	>0867	9d				_1 .byte $9D ; OPCODE FOR STA $ZZZZ,X
9	>0868	05 00				.word TEMP1 ; BYTES FOR ADDRESS OF STA
10	.086a	9d 00 d0	sta $d000,x	STA $D000,X ; CLEAR 6566 VIDEO REGISTERS
11	.086d	9d 00 d4	sta $d400,x	STA $D400,X ; CLEAR 6581 SID REGISTERS
12	.0870	e8		inx		INX
13	.0871	d0 f4		bne $0867	BNE _1
14	.0873	9a		txs		TXS ; RESET STACK POINTER
15	.0874	20 51 0d	jsr $0d51	JSR INIT_HARDWARE
16	.0877	20 72 0e	jsr $0e72	JSR INIT_GAME
17						;--------------------------------
18						; DO THE GALAXY SCREEN
19						;--------------------------------
20	.087a	20 c2 0f	jsr $0fc2	GALAXY_LOOP JSR HYPER_SPACE ; DO HYPERSPACE PICTURE
21	.087d	20 dc 0e	jsr $0edc	JSR MOVE_SCREEN ; PUT IN UNIVERSE
22	.0880	20 4d 11	jsr $114d	JSR PICK_GALAXY ; GET GALAXY CHOSEN
23	.0883	20 c2 0f	jsr $0fc2	JSR HYPER_SPACE ; DO HYPERSPACE PICTURE
24	.0886	20 dc 0e	jsr $0edc	JSR MOVE_SCREEN ; PUT IN ORBIT
25						;--------------------------------
26						; DO THE ORBIT SCREEN
27						;--------------------------------
28	.0889	20 a4 08	jsr $08a4	JSR INIT_ORBIT ; SET UP ORBIT SCREEN
29						;--------------------------------
30						; ORBIT LOOP
31						;--------------------------------
32	.088c	20 17 09	jsr $0917	ORBIT_LOOP JSR DO_SHIP ; UPDATE SHIP
33	.088f	20 4a 0c	jsr $0c4a	JSR DO_FUEL ; UPDATE FUEL
34	.0892	20 f2 0c	jsr $0cf2	JSR BLOW_UP_SHIP ; KILL SHIP IF NEEDED
35	.0895	20 8c 0b	jsr $0b8c	JSR UPDATE_SHOT ; UPDATE A SHOT
36	.0898	20 41 12	jsr $1241	JSR DO_ORBIT ; MOVE ORBIT SPRITES
37	.089b	20 49 14	jsr $1449	JSR COLLISION_DETECT ; CHECK FOR COLLISIONS
38	.089e	20 e0 23	jsr $23e0	JSR DO_SOUNDS
39	.08a1	4c 8c 08	jmp $088c	JMP ORBIT_LOOP ; DO IT AGAIN
40						;--------------------------------
41						; INITIALIZE ORBIT SCENE
42						;--------------------------------
43	.08a4	ad 16 d0	lda $d016	INIT_ORBIT LDA VIC_CONTROL_2 ; TURN ON MULTI COLOR MODE!
44	.08a7	09 10		ora #$10	ORA #$10
45	.08a9	8d 16 d0	sta $d016	STA VIC_CONTROL_2
46	.08ac	a9 08		lda #$08	LDA #$08
